# SGTest 新版重建 TODO（2026-11-15）

- GP-027-02-ATTRIBUTE-REPLAY [ ] 2026-11-25，v14 核心玩法／Server authority leaf。opcode135 配點 request 原本沒有 sequence；相同封包在尚有點數時重送可再次扣點。現改為 13-byte request（header + uint32 AttributeSequence + attribute），Client 成功送出後才遞增；Gateway 只接受同 authenticated connection 單調 sequence，replay／倒退 fail-closed，backpressure 不預先消耗序號。Release Server build 0 warning/0 error；完整 Server **342/342 PASS**，新增 replay + retained backpressure regression；Godot 4.7.2 `WIRE_CODEC_GODOT_PASS`。既有 progression gate 同時證明 points=0 超量配點不變、restart 持久化一致。HK 對非法重送的可見提示／同狀態 A/B 無直接證據，故不猜 UI；Task 保持 IN PROGRESS，不標 COMPLETE。下一安全 leaf 優先正式 combat blocker；若仍缺 cadence/v4 runtime 證據則繼續獨立 gameplay leaf。

- MVP004-PLAYER-INTERPOLATION [x] 2026-11-17：新增唯一 Renderer 路徑內的 `rendering/position_history.gd`，以既有 WorldState 的 ServerTick／MapEpoch／ConnectionEpoch 資料做**有界、只讀 presentation interpolation**；每 Actor 最多 8 sample、固定 2 Server ticks≈66.7ms、只在已收到的兩權威 sample 間內插，**不 extrapolate／不 client-predict／不回寫 authority**。跨 MapEpoch/connection generation、>8 tick gap 或不合理大 correction 立即重置，Despawn 直接移除；自身 sprite 與 camera 使用同一 sampled position，Render FPS 不驅動 Network/World。相同 30Hz 有序 jitter trace 比較：1-tick holds=76、max step=9.760；2-tick holds=26、max step=6.320；2.5-tick 無額外 smoothness gain，因此選 2-tick。GitHub Client core **43982229255 PASS**：`POSITION_HISTORY_PASS selected_delay_ticks=2 selected_delay_ms=66.7 max_samples=8 no_extrapolation=true`，paper-doll、TLS、雙 Client AOI／crossmap／NPC portal、正式 Host＋兩 main scene 全回歸 PASS。這不等於真 Internet latency、Windows GPU 體感或戰鬥動畫已驗證。

- MVP003-WORLD-READY-ACK-RESYNC [x] 2026-11-17：在**唯一 V1 compact binary / TLS / Gateway / WorldAoi** 上完成 baseline handshake 與同連線 bounded recovery，沒有第二套 transport。新增 Client→Server opcode25 `WorldReadyAck`（24B：MapEpoch+ServerTick）與 opcode26 `WorldResyncRequest`（16B：MapEpoch）；Gateway 在正式 WorldReady 入 reliable FIFO 後，只有 exact ACK 才開放 Move/NPC gameplay。合法同世代 AOI revision/action 缺口改為暫停 gameplay 並 request resync；Actor/Map/Connection identity 錯誤仍 fail-closed。Resync request 先原子關 ACK gate 再進 bounded Router channel，由**唯一 AOI pump owner**清除該 observer 尚未送出的舊 AOI、重建同 MapEpoch `WorldReady→SelfState→目前可見 Spawn`；Gateway 已排隊舊 frame 仍維持 FIFO，Client 在 resync_pending 期間忽略它們，replacement Ready 後再次 ACK 才恢復 gameplay。Server core **43988244612 PASS，92/92**，含 live bounded same-map resync／raw TLS listener ACK／Admin Host ACK；Client core **43988918797 PASS**，新增 `CLIENT_RESYNC_PASS ready_ack=true gap=delta_without_spawn resync_request=true replacement_ready_self_spawn=true reack=true gameplay_resumed=true render_loop_dependency=false`，並回歸 0/30/60/90 FPS、真 TLS、UI、雙 Client AOI、Map15↔13、NPC portal、formal Host＋兩 main scene 全 PASS。這是同 socket baseline recovery；**真正 TCP 中斷後自動重連／跨程序 crash recovery**仍另列，不把 resync 冒充 reconnect。

- MVP003-TLS-AUTO-RECONNECT [x] 2026-11-17：`ClientSession` 在**曾成功完成 WorldReady+ACK、且已選定角色**後遇到 Transport failure，最多 3 次 bounded reconnect（100/250/500ms backoff）；不重播舊 Move/NPC intents，重建全新 WorldState，沿同一 TLS/Auth/CharacterChoose 路徑自動重驗帳密／重選原角色，必須取得更高 Connection/Map generation 的 WorldReady 並再次 exact ACK 才恢復 gameplay。活動 Session 僅保留密碼 UTF-8 byte buffer 供 reconnect，stop／最終失敗立即覆零；UI reconnect 期間維持 Session 並顯示 attempt，不把暫時斷線當登出。真 TLS E2E **Client core 43990367533 PASS**：第一代 `MapEpoch=1` 從 8,8 走到 24,8 後由 fixture 強制斷 socket，Server 收到 `TransportError`、受控 release 將 24,8 寫入 SQLite；同一 Client 自動重連後 `MapEpoch=2`、從 24,8 baseline 恢復、Move sequence 重置，再走到 40,8，第二代 CleanEof，Server 最終 ActiveActors/ActiveSessions=0。Marker：`GODOT_CSHARP_TLS_RECONNECT_E2E_PASS forced_drop=true auto_reauth=true same_character=true new_generation=true persisted_position=true world_ready_reack=true bounded_attempts=3`。同一 run 亦回歸 FPS independence、same-socket resync、UI、雙 Client AOI／crossmap／NPC portal、formal Host＋兩 main scene 全 PASS。此項是短暫網路/TLS 中斷自動恢復；週期位置 checkpoint 已由 MVP003-POSITION-CHECKPOINT 補上，但 **Server process 真 hard-kill/restart E2E、長時間離線與跨程序 session ticket 尚未完成**。

- MVP003-POSITION-CHECKPOINT [x] 2026-11-17：新增唯一 Server Runtime 的 `CharacterPositionCheckpoint`，**不改 SQLite schema、不在 30Hz World owner 做 DB I/O**。WorldResultRouter 在成功保留 authoritative Changed/Lifecycle batch 後只做記憶體 Observe；每 5 秒（Host `--position-checkpoint-ms` 可設 500～60000ms）把每角色最新 ConnectionEpoch／MapEpoch／EntityRevision snapshot coalesce 成單一 SQLite transaction。Periodic flush 與 disconnect `FinalizeAsync` 共用 writer gate；final snapshot 在等待 DB 前先留下 finalized generation/revision tombstone，因此已擷取但較舊的 periodic batch 不能在 final save 後覆寫座標，嚴格更新的新連線世代才重新開啟 dirty tracking。Host supervisor 同時監看 checkpoint pump，fault 會停止服務並回報 `TLS_HOST_FAIL`；STOPPED stats 會回報 observed／periodic／final rows。Server core **43991017853 PASS，94/94**：`position checkpoint coalesces latest revision and final save cannot be regressed`、`position checkpoint bounded background loop persists dirty authoritative state` 均 PASS；Client core **43991017743 全量 PASS**，含 forced TLS reconnect、same-socket resync、雙 Client AOI／crossmap／NPC portal、formal Host＋兩 main scene。預設 5 秒 checkpoint 將**位置**的 process-crash 潛在回退範圍限制在最近成功 commit；後續 Server core **43991613110 PASS** 已加入真 Linux process `SIGKILL` Gate：40,8 已由背景 checkpoint commit 後，World 再移動到尚未 checkpoint 的 24,8，父程序立即 hard-kill，新的 verifier process 重開同一 SQLite 後得到 40,8（`CHECKPOINT_PROCESS_KILL_E2E_PASS signal=SIGKILL durable=40,8 volatile_after_checkpoint=24,8 reopened=40,8 db_schema_unchanged=true`）。同 commit 的 Client core **43991612928 全量 PASS**，含 forced TLS reconnect／雙 Client AOI／crossmap／NPC portal／formal Host+兩 main scene。此 Gate 證明最近已 commit **position** checkpoint 可跨 process kill/reopen 保留；仍不能用來代表 combat／drop／物品／金錢等可靠結算 durability，也不宣稱 kill 正好發生在 SQLite transaction 中途的 storage-fault 測試。

## MVP 主線：Map15 主城 + Map13 台地，兩圖功能完整（全部未完成）

**先驗證兩張真地圖，再走路／多人 AOI／雙向傳點、完整玩法與多怪／多掉落**；Map15 以城市互動為主，Map13 以多人／AI／戰鬥壓測為主；實際原版人口／NPC／傳點均需原始資料核實。兩圖互通是 MVP 必要條件，**不再將跨圖實測排到第二階段**。每一項有原版行為、Server Authority、錯誤／多人競態與回歸測試；存在多種可行架構時先做 ALT001 比較，選最優解後再繼續。

- MVP001 [ ] **Map15 + Map13 原始資料／素材驗證**：確認原版兩圖 Map/Scenery/Blocking/NPC/Enemy／傳點資料；唯一 Converter 分圖定向生成必要資產及 Server authoritative map content；兩次 --map build 需避免輸出互相覆蓋／污染，驗證 SHA256、Godot Atlas/ResourceLoader/PCK，原版素材不得上傳公開 Git。
  - MVP001-MAP-DATA [x] 2026-11-15 原始 Data1.dat（41,687 entries + 41,707 overlays）解包、雙圖 `world-content --include-map --map 13 --map 15` 私有內容輸出 PASS：Map13 866 raw tile ID（含原始 -1）／367 Scenery／3 NPC，Map15 976 raw tile ID／1,848 Scenery／52 NPC；原版兩張 Blocking 與 Old Server Dat 同圖 SHA256 一致；兩張 terrain PNG 像素往返 PASS；檔案反向建置重跑 10/10 SHA256 一致；共 **53 項現行 Python 測試 PASS**。後續原版研究已撤回「缺Z」推論：Map15有15筆子物件，world v3保留parent_index／child_count；見MVP002-SCENERY-HIERARCHY。此項只驗證**地圖／NPC 來源內容**，不是整個 MVP001。
  - MVP001-TERRAIN-SOURCE-INDEX [x] 2026-11-16 使用者回報 Map15 部分地磚看似錯位後重新核對原版執行檔，確認問題不是 row-major XY 擺放，而是先前 Converter 取錯 PIC source index：`CELMap2D::ReadTileArray`/`DrawTile` 對 MapBlock signed int16 **先 +1**，再由 `CDDxTile::Convert` 以該 ushort index 讀取已略過 8-byte header 的 32×32 RGB565 PIC payload；所以 raw 0→PIC1、raw 1→PIC2、raw -1/0xffff→PIC0，**65535 不是空格**。舊版直接用 `PIC[raw]` 並跳過 65535，Map15 230,400/230,400 格都會選到不同來源 tile，Map13 230,393/230,400 格亦不同。已改唯一 `build_maps.py`、Runtime manifest `tile_source_rule=int16_plus_one_wrap`、AssetStore/TerrainData fail-closed 舊輸出，新增 source-level synthetic regression。實跑：Python **82/82 PASS**；私有原始 Map13/15 runtime-only 重建 PASS（Map13 866、Map15 976 raw ID；pixel round-trip=true）；新 PCK ResourceLoader **256 Texture / 508 codec extents PASS**，PCK SHA256=`5ecee8d59fcde47554766b01c9c752b0c43784ef9ec1dafc6cf6ff2c0638c1ac`；Godot 原生 Terrain 雙圖720p/1080p **16 capture 全部 0 pixel mismatch**；World-view 12 capture／38 原始 SPR frame PASS、資源最後全0；World Canvas 1輪 PASS。私有 PCK/capture/report 不進 Git。這項修正來源 tile 契約，不代表 MVP002 完整角色／多人 E2E 已完成。
  - MVP001-PORTAL-EVIDENCE [x] 靜態原版腳本核實 Map15 NPC spawn_id=46 → Map13 (2560,2880)；Map13 NPC spawn_id=1 → Map15 (7872,11296)；Map15 48 個 NPC 仍需動態 Quest/Shop/Bank 等原版規則研究，**runtime transfer NOT VERIFIED**。
  - MVP001-ENEMY-SOURCE [x] 2026-11-15 原版 `Map13/Enemy.dat` 78 Spawn／9 種、`Map15/Enemy.dat` 4 Spawn／1 種；合計 10 種 Enemy 的完整原版 ACT／SPR 五動作、8 邏輯方向與原始來源 SHA 驗證 PASS，合計 557 Frame；已將 `--include-population` 整合唯一 world-content CLI，分圖輸出 `population_evidence.json`，不編造 HP/ATK/AI 或假 Atlas；雙圖反向重跑 2/2 SHA 相同。正式 Height-Dense Builder 使用真實來源試轉 10/10 獨立 Atlas、10 頁／557 Frame PASS（**僅私有預覽，尚未正式發布**）；現行 57 項 Python 測試 PASS。此項僅為 source evidence／atlas smoke，不是正式遊戲 Spawn、Client/Server E2E 或完整 Archive PASS。

- MVP006-MONSTER-AUTHORITY-FOUNDATION [x] 2026-11-17：建立**唯一 map-local Monster authority**，不沿用舊 prototype 的 per-session 怪物複本。正式 Server 只接受 `version=4 / kind=verified_monster_runtime / stats_verified=true / gameplay_ready=true` 的 population；既有 converter v3 visual population（`stats=null`）一律不能進 Combat。Spawn 必須落在同一 Blocking 可走格；AI 以單一 30Hz World clock 驅動，map-local `MonsterRuntime` 有每步怪物數／A* 查詢／展開格硬上限與 round-robin fairness，實測 300 Enemy、cap64 不全掃；包含 Idle/Wander/Chase/Attack/Dead、Blocking/A*、Aggro/LoseTarget/Leash、server-only trusted damage、Death/Respawn。原始 `Enemy_Data.csv` 僅把已交叉驗證的 **Level=col3、MaxHP=col9** 保存成 source evidence；Damage/Aggro/Range/Cooldown 仍不標成原版事實。Asset converter CI **44019018986 PASS**；Server core **44021869726 PASS，101/101**，含 verified gate、blocked spawn、300 Enemy fair budget、walk/chase/attack、shared 30Hz idle clock、live Host→Router→MonsterRuntime→AOI→Gateway。此子項不是完整 Combat：玩家 HP/ATK/DEF、玩家打怪、怪打玩家扣血、MISS、技能、獎勵/Drop 尚未完成。

- MVP006-MONSTER-WIRE-AOI-PRESENTATION [x] 2026-11-17：在既有 **V1 compact binary / WorldAoi / Gateway reliable FIFO / bounded resync / WorldView** 上完成怪物同步與真正呈現，沒有第二套 AOI/transport。新增 opcode27 `MonsterSpawn`、28 `MonsterDelta`（78B）與29 `MonsterDespawn`（40B）；Client 用獨立 `WorldState.monsters`，identity/revision gap 走既有 same-MapEpoch resync。玩家與怪物共用同一 AOI enter/exit hysteresis、visible cap、reliable FIFO 與 resync baseline；同 World Tick 先 stage 最終 monster snapshot，再套 Player Join/Move，避免新 observer 收到 stale Spawn+same-tick Delta。正式 Host 只有找到 verified v4 population 才啟用 Monster runtime，且只在此時 `PublishIdleTicks=true`；純玩家 Host 保持 event-only。Client `MonsterPresentation` 只讀 v3 **visual** population，完全忽略 combat stats；依已驗證 ACT 語意使用 Idle=S→NS、Wander/Chase=W→NW、Attack=A→FA、Dead=D→ND，H→NH 保留給真正受擊事件，並用既有 PositionHistory／ActionTimeline／WorldDrawList 與玩家一起插入景物排序。Client core **44021869721 PASS**：`MONSTER_PRESENTATION_PASS visual_population_only=true stats_ignored=true NS_idle=true NW_move=true FA_attack=true ND_death=true NH_reserved=true interpolation=true common_draw_order=true`；formal Host＋兩個真 main scene 都得到 `ONLINE_MAIN_GATE_MONSTER ... count=1 presented=1`，最終 `ONLINE_MAIN_SCENE_FORMAL_HOST_GATE_PASS ... monster_state=true ...`，同 run 的 TLS reconnect／雙 Client AOI／Map15↔13／NPC portal 全 PASS。公開 Gate 使用明確 `test_only=true` synthetic v4 stats；**不代表私人原版 Damage/Aggro/Range 數值已核實**。

- MVP006-NORMAL-ATTACK [x] 2026-11-17：完成**玩家→怪物普通攻擊的第一個 Server-authoritative 垂直切片**，沿用唯一 V1 compact binary／TLS／Gateway／Router／WorldAoi／MonsterRuntime，不新增第二套 combat transport。Client opcode5 `NormalAttack` 固定 28B，只帶 `MapEpoch + AttackSequence + TargetMonsterEntityId`，**不含 ActorId／ConnectionEpoch／Attack／damage／range**；登入選角時 Gateway 從 SQLite trusted `CharacterCombatProfile` 綁定 Attack/Hit/Dodge/Range/Cooldown，無 profile 明確回 `CombatUnavailable`。bounded Router attack channel 由唯一 AOI/Monster coordinator writer 按權威玩家座標、同 MapEpoch、Blocking direct-clear、trusted AttackRange、server-tick cooldown、sequence replay guard 結算；成功後只呼叫既有 `MonsterRuntime.ApplyTrustedDamage`，並把 HP/Death 透過共享 `MonsterDelta` 發給 AOI。opcode6 `NormalAttackResult` 57B 是 requester reliable result，可供 Damage/MISS/UI；Godot reducer **不以 receipt 修改怪物 HP**，只以 MonsterDelta 更新 authority。Server core **44038307685 PASS，108/108**：含 request 無 client damage/range、Ready ACK gate、trusted profile injection、range/cooldown/replay/HP/death/shared AOI；Client core **44038307692 全量 PASS**：`NORMAL_ATTACK_GODOT_PASS ... delta_owns_hp=true ...`、真 TLS `CLIENT_COMBAT_TLS_PASS target=1001500001 damage=60 hp=40 request_has_no_damage=true ...`、`GODOT_CSHARP_TLS_COMBAT_E2E_PASS ...`，並回歸 reconnect、雙 Client、Map15↔13、NPC portal、formal Host＋兩 main scene。測試的 Attack=60／Range=48 來自明確 `SyntheticTest` profile；目前 damage=trusted Attack 是相容性 baseline，**原版玩家 damage/DEF/MISS/crit 公式尚未核實，MISS status 只保留 protocol 能力而不憑空擲骰**。

- MVP006-MONSTER-TO-PLAYER-DAMAGE [x] 2026-11-17：完成**怪物→玩家權威傷害／HP／Death durable pipeline**。新增唯一 `PlayerCombatSettlement` bounded single-writer：Monster/AOI thread 只以 trusted Monster Attack event + 已登入 combat profile Defense 做 projected HP reservation（目前相容性 baseline=`max(1, AttackDamage-Defense/5)`），**不做 SQLite I/O**；projected HP 到 0 後同 Tick 後續 hit 立即拒絕，避免 overkill/重複 death。背景 worker 以 SQLite transaction 原子扣 HP，只有 commit 成功且實際 Damage/HP 與 reservation 完全一致後，才把 opcode8 `PlayerDamage` 57B 放進該 Gateway reliable FIFO；drain completion 在 reliable queue 成功後才完成。CharacterChoose 有 combat profile 時 opcode7 `PlayerCombatState` 24B 在 WorldReady 前提供 HP baseline；沒有 profile 不創造預設 combat state，Monster HP owner 視為不可攻擊。Settlement 初始 suspended，**只有 Gateway route attach 成功後才 activation**，避免 DB 已扣血但 socket 尚未可投遞的 race；NPC transfer 與 disconnect/reconnect 先 suspend/close + drain，再改 MapEpoch／detach，因此舊世代 committed damage 不會落到新地圖。Godot reducer 驗證 DamageSequence／MapEpoch／ServerTick、duplicate stale、sequence gap fail-closed、Died 必須等於 HP=0；死亡後 Move/NPC/NormalAttack 在 ClientSession **送包前**即拒絕。正式 Host supervisor 監看 settlement worker，任何 commit/delivery divergence fault service。Server core **44041147095 PASS，114/114**；Client core **44041146895 全量 PASS**：`PLAYER_DAMAGE_GODOT_PASS baseline_bytes=24 damage_bytes=57 ... death_gates_intents=true`，真 TLS `CLIENT_COMBAT_TLS_PASS ... player_damage=60 monster_hp=40 monster_damage=18 player_hp=82 ... damage_after_db_commit=true` 與 `GODOT_CSHARP_TLS_COMBAT_E2E_PASS ... monster_hit_committed_before_player_damage=true authoritative_player_hp=true`，並回歸 reconnect、雙 Client、crossmap、NPC portal、formal Host＋兩 main scene。測試 raw MonsterAttack=20／Defense=10→18 來自明確 SyntheticTest compatibility rule；**原版怪打玩家 damage/DEF/MISS/crit 公式仍未核實**。目前 Client dead gate 已有，但 Server World 既有 route／惡意 Move/NPC 的死亡 gate 仍是下一切片。

- MVP006-SERVER-DEATH-GATE [x] 2026-11-17：補齊死亡後的 **Server-authoritative gameplay gate**，不是只靠 Client UI 阻擋。`ServerWorld` 以 trusted `CombatDead` 狀態凍結既有 route；惡意／舊 Client 直接送 Move 會得到 `MoveReceiptStatus.CombatDead=11` 且座標不變，NPC portal 回 `NpcPortalStatus.CombatDead=6`，玩家普通攻擊在 combat owner 看到 HP=0 時回 `CombatUnavailable`。lethal SQLite commit 後先由 trusted lifecycle 將 World actor 標死，再投遞 reliable `PlayerDamage(Died=true)`，所以死亡 frame 不會早於 Server gate。登入持久 HP=0 的角色也會在 WorldReady 前恢復 dead gate。修正 `CombatDead=11` encode/decode 上限不一致與 TLS combat revision 過度嚴格回歸後，Server／Client core 於 **0a611b36** 全綠。

- MVP006-PLAYER-RESPAWN [x] 2026-11-17：完成**玩家死亡→耐久復活→重新開放 gameplay** 第一個權威切片。新增 opcode30 `PlayerRespawn` 20B，只帶 `MapEpoch + RespawnSequence`，**不含 ActorId／HP／座標／dead=false**；Gateway 注入 authenticated ActorId/ConnectionEpoch。SQLite 新增唯一 trusted `HP=0 → MaxHP` 原子 restore，已存活／無 combat profile 一律 fail closed；`PlayerCombatSettlement` 先 suspend + drain pending damage，再 durable restore，成功後仍保持 untargetable。Server 接著解除 World `CombatDead`，先把 opcode31 `PlayerRespawnResult` 29B 放入同一 reliable FIFO，**最後才重新啟用 Monster targetability**，避免復活後第一個 Damage 越過 respawn baseline。Godot 只在本角色確實 dead 時送 respawn，accepted result 恢復 HP/dead gate，但同一 MapEpoch 的 `DamageSequence` **不歸零**；復活後 Move 再次由真 Server 接受。位置目前保留死亡時 authoritative 座標，明確是 provisional in-place policy，**不是宣稱原版 Reborn/SavePoint 規則已驗證**。Server core **44049150018 PASS，121/121**；Client core **44049150061 PASS**：`PLAYER_DAMAGE_GODOT_PASS ... respawn_reopens_intents=true damage_sequence_continuous=true`、真 TLS `CLIENT_COMBAT_TLS_PASS ... death_hp=0 respawn_hp=100 dead=false ... move_after_respawn=true`，並回歸 reconnect、雙 Client、Map15↔13、NPC portal、formal Host＋兩 main scene。

## 舊 REB 任務依賴對照

原 REB003 是全素材 Gate、REB019 是正式 Wire 全量選型、REB010～023 是 Client/Server 核心；REB024～027 原列第二階段的**系統邏輯**已被提前納入 MVP005～006，只有全原版資料內容／跨圖 E2E 才留 MVP009。REB030～032 全量壓測與發行仍在 MVP 後進行。**舊 REB 編號保留方便追蹤，但不得用「第二階段」說法延後 MVP 必要功能。**

## 每個開發任務的選型門檻（長期規則，非單次完成事項）

**多種做法 → 先比較 → 用相同資料／負載實測 → 選最佳整體解 → 整合唯一正式程式 → 更新文件／TODO → 再繼續下一步。** 依 AGENTS.md 與 docs/TEST_PLAN.md 的 ALT001；正確性、安全、可靠事件先過關，再看體感 P95/P99、CPU/RSS/VRAM、網路流量／GC、維護成本。沒明顯差距選簡單方案。隔離基準不等於 E2E；未測標註 NOT VERIFIED。沒有實質替代方案的小改動直接做必要回歸；禁止無止盡 Benchmark。

## Server 新版（離線 30Hz Host 與基礎核心已實作；網路／完整遊戲仍未完成）

- REB020 [ ] Server/Godot TLS Auth/CharacterChoose/Move/MoveReceipt/WorldReady real loopback E2E PASS, with WorldResultRouter + WorldAoiPump and no competing transport architecture. Still required: production listener/certificate/admission + rate limits, app login UI, WorldReady ACK/resync, position-save lifecycle, correct two-client AOI visual E2E and actual Map15/13 multi-entity gameplay.
- REB020-FRAMING-PROBE [x] 2026-11-16 test-only TCP framing candidate added to the existing `tools/tests/wire_aoi_probe`; C#/.NET 8 and Godot 4.7.2 both PASS: 33 byte-split boundaries, 100 seeded fragmented/coalesced streams (3 frames each), 5 invalid lengths, 3 invalid version/opcode/payload widths, partial-frame reset, valid-then-invalid ordering. C# buffer bounded at 64 bytes during fuzz; Python unit suite 81 PASS. This remains test evidence only; formal runtime codec is recorded by REB020-CODEC-SELECT.
- REB020-CODEC-SELECT [x] 2026-11-16 WIRE001 first-stage selection complete: deterministic 4,000-event Move/Combat/Drop/Spawn trace compared real MessagePack 3.1.12, Google.Protobuf 3.36.2 and compact binary under the same 8-byte header. Average framed bytes/event compact=35.40, MessagePack=28.61, Protobuf=29.39; .NET compact encode/decode P50=0.080/0.118µs, MessagePack=0.273/0.382µs, Protobuf=0.230/0.287µs; Godot 4.7.2 Move fixture compact=1.205/0.922µs, MessagePack test codec=2.135/3.506µs, Protobuf test codec=4.021/5.278µs. All candidates round-trip and cross-runtime golden fixtures PASS. - REB020-GATEWAY-BOUNDARY [x] 2026-11-16 production transport boundary core added without opening a socket: `GatewayConnection` is created from a trusted `ActorSnapshot`, so raw Client Move bytes can only supply MapEpoch/MovementSequence/target while ActorId and ConnectionEpoch come from the bound connection. World `MoveReceipt` now carries ActorId + ConnectionEpoch + MapEpoch, preventing a delayed receipt from an old login from being routed to a newer connection. `WireStreamParser` uses fixed-capacity buffering, validates length/version/opcode, handles split/coalesced frames, retains a complete frame when World ingress backpressures, and fails closed on overflow/protocol errors. Per-connection reliable egress is bounded by frame count + bytes, FIFO, never DropOldest; stale connection/future-map routing is rejected, while an old-map receipt on the same connection remains tagged for Client discard. A map transfer may only monotonically update MapEpoch on the same connection; reconnect requires a NEW GatewayConnection. GitHub Actions `.github/workflows/server-core.yml` now builds Server + Host and runs synthetic checks on Ubuntu 24.04/.NET 8; first run exposed 6 pre-existing nullable warnings in `WorldHostChecks`, fixed without relaxing TreatWarningsAsErrors. Final GitHub CI: build 0 warning/error, **36/36 PASS**. Still **NO Socket/TLS/Auth credentials/WorldReady/ACK/reconnect or real Client E2E**; REB020/MVP003 remain [ ].

No listener or fake credentials are exposed by the production Host. Reliable egress is awaitable without polling; World ingress backpressure retains the same complete Move and retries with bounded delay/time budget. - REB023-IDENTITY-MIGRATION-BACKUP [x] 2026-11-20：完成 **SQLite identity schema migration／backup safety policy**，不改目前 schema v21、不新增第二套 DB。任何已存在且可辨識的舊 schema 在 `InitializeAsync()` 真正修改前，先透過 SQLite Backup API 產生同目錄 `identity.db.pre-v<old>-to-v21.<UTC>.<GUID>.bak` 一致快照；手動本機 `identity-backup --identity <DB> --output <NEW_DB>` 則明確**不初始化／不升級來源**，適合停服後做 operator 備份。備份 destination 不可等於來源、既有檔不覆寫；完成後執行 `PRAGMA integrity_check`、比對來源/備份 schema version，並回傳 bytes＋SHA-256。回歸驗證 live WAL source 在 account A 後備份、再建立 account B，snapshot 僅含 A 而 source 含 A+B；另 stage v20→v21 驗證升級前留下獨立可開啟、schema=20、資料完整的 pre-migration backup，來源才升為 v21；CLI 備份可重新開啟並驗證帳密，重複目的地與 source==destination 均拒絕。主要 Commit `ac47e479`；Server Core **44766021159 PASS，252/252**，包含三個新增 backup/migration Gate 與既有 hard-kill checkpoint regression。**此項是單機 DB migration safety，不等於 off-host rotation／retention／encryption／災難復原演練。**

- REB023-AUTH-ADMISSION-RATE-POLICY [x] 2026-11-20：完成 **formal Host bounded admission + authentication brute-force rate policy** 收斂。既有 listener global／per-IP concurrent caps 與 TLS/auth timeout 維持；Host 的 `--auth-failures-per-address`、`--auth-failures-per-account`、`--auth-failure-window-seconds`、`--auth-lockout-seconds` 原本雖有讀值卻未列入合法 CLI options，現已可實際部署並由真 Host process Gate 驗證。AuthenticationRateLimiter 維持 address＋normalized account 雙層、bounded tracking、InvalidCredentials 泛化回覆；本輪修正容量全由 active lockout bucket 佔滿時的 LRU 繞過：**不得為新 key 驅逐仍在生效的 lockout**，而是啟用 bounded scope-wide lockout 時窗 fail-closed，時間到再恢復；非 blocked 舊 bucket 仍可 LRU eviction，避免記憶體無界。Commit `74b8f99c`；Server Core **44767204976 PASS、253/253**，新增 saturation bypass regression 與 Host auth-policy CLI Gate；Client Core **44767205009 PASS**（wire/client 未修改）。**此 V1 policy 是單 Server process 的 admission／brute-force safety，不宣稱取代防火牆、反 DDoS、跨節點共享封鎖或 process-restart 持久 lockout。**

- REB023-PUBLIC-ACCOUNT-REGISTRATION [x] 2026-11-20：完成 **預設關閉、明示啟用的公開帳號註冊垂直切片**，不把未知帳號的 AuthRequest 偷偷轉成建帳。compact-binary V1 使用 opcode17 AccountRegisterRequest（username/password，仍只允許 TLS）與 opcode18 AccountRegisterResult（Created／Unavailable／UsernameTaken／InvalidInput／RateLimited）。Formal Host 預設 `--account-registration disabled`；啟用後由獨立 `AccountRegistrationRateLimiter` 以 canonical source IP 做 bounded quota（預設3次/10分鐘、4096 IP），每次請求不論成功/重複/非法都消耗 quota，容量滿 fail-closed，避免用成功註冊繞過登入失敗 limiter。**Created 只建立 durable credentials，絕不授權 gameplay**：同一 TLS 還必須再送既有 AuthRequest，成功後才拿 CharacterList；Client 只有明確「建立帳號並登入」按鈕會走 `register_secure`，普通登入失敗不會自動註冊，Created 後 reconnect 也不重建帳號。Server 真 TLS Gate 驗證 default-disabled 不改 DB、enabled 建帳→同 TLS Auth、duplicate、第三次 quota RateLimited 且 DB/World 不變；Server Core **44769015052 PASS 256/256**。Godot codec／WorldState／UI targeted Gate 與真 C# TLS fixture 驗證 `CLIENT_REGISTRATION_TLS_PASS created=true authenticated=true character_list_empty=true gameplay_authority=false`、`REGISTER_FIXTURE_PASS ... characters=0 active=0`；Client Core **44770285345 PASS**，且 reconnect／登入登出 UI／雙 Client AOI／Map15↔13／NPC portal／Formal Host＋兩 actual main-scene 均維持 PASS。主要 Commit `be65a4b9`、`3eb43906`、fixture `b3f15c49`、opcode allowlist 修正 `13a466cd`。**此功能不是 email 驗證、CAPTCHA、帳號復原、跨節點註冊配額或 Internet WAF/DDoS 防護。**

- REB023-IDENTITY-STORE [x] 2026-11-16: added `Identity/PasswordHasher.cs` and `Identity/SqliteIdentityStore.cs`. Password format is Argon2id v19 PHC-style with bounded concurrency; selected policy is m=9216 KiB,t=4,p=1 after target-Windows comparison against OWASP-equivalent Argon2id profiles and PBKDF2 fallback evidence. SQLite schema v1 uses WAL, foreign keys, unique normalized username, unique `(account_id,slot)` and character name, explicit schema-version fail-closed, deterministic DB ownership (pooling disabled), and keeps all DB/KDF work outside the 30Hz World thread. Missing-account auth still runs a dummy Argon2id verify; disabled account status is only returned after correct password. CharacterId is persistent and suitable for later trusted ActorId binding, but raw Client never supplies it. Tests cover salted password roundtrip/wrong password/hostile KDF params, case-stable account identity + no plaintext password in SQLite files, character slots/name normalization/reopen persistence, concurrent same-slot single winner, disabled-account behavior, and schema mismatch. Local Release: Server build 0 warnings/errors, Host build 0 warnings/errors, **46/46 Server checks PASS**. `CharacterChoose` wire/TLS binding is the next slice; REB020/REB023/MVP003 remain incomplete.

- REB020-AUTH-CHARACTER-WIRE [x] 2026-11-16: compact-binary V1 adds TLS-only opcodes AuthRequest(10), AuthResult(11), CharacterList(12), CharacterChoose(13), CharacterChooseResult(14). `IdentityTlsAuthorization` reads credentials only after mandatory TLS, authenticates via `SqliteIdentityStore`, sends only characters owned by that account, rejects foreign CharacterId, then calls `ServerWorldHost.TrustedRegisterAsync(persistent character_id, stored map/x/y)` and constructs `GatewayConnection` from the returned trusted `ActorSnapshot`; gameplay Client still never supplies ActorId/ConnectionEpoch. Successful choose returns authoritative map/x/y + MapEpoch needed by MoveTarget. Auth/character frames sent after gameplay binding are rejected by Gateway. TLS pre-gameplay authorization timeout was raised from machine-only 5s to configurable 120s default (bounded max 10min) so human CharacterChoose is possible; handshake remains 5s. - REB010-GODOT-TLS-LOGIN [x] 2026-11-16: Godot `network/wire_codec.gd` now matches server V1 identity frames in the Client-used directions (encode AuthRequest/CharacterChoose/MoveTarget; decode AuthResult/CharacterList/CharacterChooseResult/MoveReceipt) with strict size/status checks and cross-runtime golden fixtures. Added `network/client_transport.gd`: TCP/TLS lives on a dedicated `Thread`, not `_process`; production `start_secure()` uses `TLSOptions.client()` / optional trusted chain, while `start_test_unsafe()` is debug-build-only. TCP/TLS are polled on the worker; send uses nonblocking `put_partial_data`; receive uses `get_partial_data`; outbound, event and receive buffers are bounded and fail closed instead of DropOldest. A review caught and fixed prefix-consumption logic and `set_no_delay` ordering before merge. Real Windows Godot 4.7.2 <-> .NET fixture E2E uses a generated self-signed public cert explicitly loaded as a trusted chain (not unsafe TLS), then performs Auth -> CharacterList -> CharacterChoose -> Move; Godot reports sent=3/received=3 and Server confirms accepted actor/MapEpoch/sequence. This proves the socket/TLS/login worker path, NOT full render-FPS independence: the future non-render WorldState/reducer must drain events and be tested at render=0/30/60/90. REB010/MVP003 remain [ ].

- REB010-WORLDSTATE-FPS [x] 2026-11-16: added pure `network/world_state.gd` and threaded `network/client_session.gd`. Transport owns socket/TLS; Session owns protocol intents/reducer on a separate non-render Thread; presentation reads immutable snapshot copies only. Auth/CharacterList/Choose/MoveReceipt state transitions are explicit; future MapEpoch receipt fails closed, duplicate/old sequence is ignored/counted, receipts for unsent sequence are rejected. Intents are bounded and never silently dropped; choose/move remain queued until their legal state and transport capacity allow sending. `test_client_fps_independence.gd` runs the identical scripted Auth->Choose->Move trace at render polling cadences 0/30/60/90; all four produce identical final state and exact outbound wire order, including the 0-render case where no snapshot polling occurs during the work window.
